CrashBench / SafeLIBERO · 论文方向底稿 · 2026-11-02

CrashBench / SafeLIBERO · paper-direction brief · 2026-11-02

人在环论文方向

HIL Paper Directions

导师的题目是"VLA 或 VLM 在人在环（human in the loop）里有多鲁棒"。这页把仓库到 10-31 为止的结果和文献定位，整理成可以拿去讨论的几个论文方向，排出先后，并把推荐的前两篇写成"如果能行，最后的论文长什么样"。

The advisor's question is "how robust is a VLA or a VLM in human-in-the-loop settings". This page turns the repo's results and literature placement (as of 31 Oct) into paper directions to discuss, ranks them, and sketches what the two recommended papers would look like if they work.

依据：分支 hil-suite（f5f0964）的报告与冻结读数Basis: reports and frozen readings on branch hil-suite (f5f0964) 流程：三轮多代理（6 读者 → 7 提案 + 21 核查 → 2 篇成稿样貌各经 3 位批评者）Process: three multi-agent rounds (6 readers → 7 proposers + 21 verifiers → 2 sketches, 3 critics each) 配套文档：docs/generative_verifier/PAPER_DIRECTIONS.mdCompanion doc: docs/generative_verifier/PAPER_DIRECTIONS.md
结论Summary 三问两篇Three questions, two papers 七个方向Seven directions 时间表Timeline 论文 1 样貌Paper 1 sketch 论文 2 样貌Paper 2 sketch 折叠的方向Folded directions 要你决定的事Decisions needed 给导师的英文要点Talking points 怎么来的How this was made

0 · 一句话结论

0 · Summary

先写论文 1（VLA 半边）："停"停不下来——完成原任务是这些检查点的吸引子。两个公开的 benchmark 微调 VLA 对三句没训练过的良性口令（停、回起点、取消）几乎从不执行，pi0.5（一个 SafeLIBERO 任务组合）对中途加的空间规则也一样，原任务照常完成；从一开始就说"停"也不停，只是会把任务带偏（完成 0.559 / 0.661）：越晚说，话的分量越轻。被推、被挪、被夺走物体后 pi0.5 也照样完成任务——这是"状态反应性"对照，不是不对称（第三轮领域主席：两者观察的是同一种行为）。只有训练过的句子能在早期把它拉出来。证据齐全、读数冻结，RSS 2027 扩展摘要（12-04）零新 GPU 即可投，同周挂 arXiv 占时间戳；三个小对照（≈ 4.5–6.5 GPU h）把"中途"二字坐实。

Write Paper 1 first (the VLA half): "stop" doesn't stop: finishing the original task is the attractor of these checkpoints. Two public benchmark-fine-tuned VLAs almost never act on three untrained benign commands (stop, return, cancel), pi0.5 (one SafeLIBERO composition) does not act on a spatial rule added mid-task either, and the original task is still completed; "stop" said from the first step never halts either, it only derails the task (completion 0.559 / 0.661): the later the words come, the less they weigh. Pushed, moved or robbed of the object, pi0.5 also keeps finishing the task, which is a state-reactivity control, not an asymmetry (the round-3 area chair: both observe the same behaviour). Only a trained sentence pulls the policy out, and only early. The evidence is complete and frozen; the RSS 2027 extended abstract (4 Dec) needs no new GPU time, with arXiv v1 the same week as the time-stamp; three small controls (≈ 4.5–6.5 GPU h) would make "mid-task" a measured word.

再写论文 2（VLM 半边）：现成 VLM 在去掉文字捷径的反事实孪生上判断人的中途规则只有 0.50，直接训练判决在孪生上也只有 0.50（自然布局上按种子 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316，不能进标题）。这个预算下只有两种"先写出定位"的目标过了孪生测试：定位 + 程序在未见初始状态上 0.933（3 种子），定位 + 自己算 0.923（1 种子，仅验证集）。离线放在策略前面，违规 60/60 → 5/60，代价 59/60 → 52/60（离线时 = 判官准确率的算术）。三个生死测试先跑（≈ 73–90 GPU h，每项需批准）；CoRL 2027 只在 12 月中前加第二类规则或在线门控时成立，否则 IROS / RA-L 为主。

Then Paper 2 (the VLM half): off-the-shelf VLMs judge a human's mid-task rule at 0.50 on counterfactual twins that remove the text shortcut, and a direct-verdict fine-tune also sits at 0.50 on twins (0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316 on natural layouts by seed, so that number cannot carry a title). At this budget only the two targets that write positions pass the twins: positions + a program 0.933 on unseen initial states (3 seeds), positions + own computation 0.923 (1 seed, validation only). Composed offline in front of the policy it cuts violations 60/60 → 5/60 at a cost of 59/60 → 52/60 (offline, that is arithmetic on judge accuracy). Three kill tests run first (≈ 73–90 GPU h, each needing a go-ahead); CoRL 2027 holds only if a second rule family or the online gate is added by mid-December, otherwise IROS / RA-L leads.

其余五个方向（门控系统的执行器层、机制、"什么话能让它停"、ML 分解、benchmark）都折进前两篇或暂缓。

The other five directions (the gate system's executor tier, mechanism, "can anything you say stop it", ML decomposition, benchmark) fold into the two papers or are held.

≤ 2 / 236中途口令被执行的次数（每格，两策略）mid-task commands acted on, per cell, both policies
0.93–1.00pi0.5 被推、被挪后仍完成任务（÷ 对照；状态反应性对照）pi0.5 still finishes the task after pushes and moves (÷ control; the state-reactivity control)
0.50 → 0.93判官：直接判决 → 定位 + 程序（未见初始状态）judge: direct verdict → positions + program (unseen initial states)
60 → 5 / 60离线门控后的不可行规则穿越infeasible-rule crossings after the offline gate

成本到今天：HIL 套件 25.30 GPU h + SafeLIBERO 三连 9.31 GPU h，API $0；验证器 V4 约 17.7、V5 约 40 GPU h，API $0.53。推荐路线到明年 4 月：最少约 80–95 GPU h（论文 1 的三个小对照 ≈ 4.5–6.5 + 论文 2 的三个生死测试 ≈ 73–90），全批约 130–160；$0（可选 GPT-6 Luna 对照：V5 孪生 ≈ $3.7，直接 + 事实）。

Cost to date: HIL suite 25.30 GPU h + SafeLIBERO trio 9.31 GPU h, $0 API; verifier V4 about 17.7 and V5 about 40 GPU h, $0.53 API. The recommended path through April is about 80–95 GPU h at minimum (Paper 1's three small controls ≈ 4.5–6.5 + Paper 2's three kill tests ≈ 73–90) and about 130–160 if every addition is approved, at $0 (an optional GPT-6 Luna control would cost about $3.7 on the V5 twins, direct + facts).

1 · 三个问题，两篇论文

1 · Three questions, two papers

仓库里有四条线：标准 LIBERO 上的 HIL 套件（pi0.5 + OpenVLA-OFT；pi0 卡在环境门槛）、SafeLIBERO 上的三连（中途线规则、回起点、需要绕路的禁区）加离线门控、生成式验证器 V4/V5（Qwen2.5-VL-3B + LoRA 的四种训练目标和反事实孪生）、以及 CrashBench 的八个保留结果（储备）。10-28 定下的故事线是三个问题，前两篇论文各占一半。第三轮批评后两处措辞变了：问 1 的物理半边是"状态反应性对照"而不是不对称；问 3 的离线门控只是判官准确率的算术，真正的闭环要等在线门控。

The repo holds four lines: the HIL suite on standard LIBERO (pi0.5 + OpenVLA-OFT; pi0 stopped at the environment gate), the SafeLIBERO trio (mid-task line rule, return-to-start, a keep-out needing a detour) plus the offline gate, the generative verifier V4/V5 (four training targets for Qwen2.5-VL-3B + LoRA and counterfactual twins), and CrashBench's eight retained results (reserve). The storyline fixed on 28 Oct is three questions; the two papers take one half each. Two wordings changed after round 3: the physical half of Q1 is a state-reactivity control, not an asymmetry, and the offline gate of Q3 is arithmetic on judge accuracy until an online gate runs.

2 · 七个候选方向，三视角核查后的排名

2 · Seven candidate directions, ranked after a three-lens check

七个角度各自提方向，合并去重成七个；每个方向由"新颖性 N / 可行性 F / 审稿人 R"三位核查者独立打分（1–5），综合 = 0.4 R + 0.35 N + 0.25 F。排名者对第 1 名上调了 0.35：三位核查者都说它的致命问题"免费改标题就能解决"。

Seven angles each proposed directions, merged into seven; three verifiers (novelty N, feasibility F, reviewer R) scored each independently on 1–5, composite = 0.4 R + 0.35 N + 0.25 F. The ranker raised the first by 0.35 because all three verifiers said its fatal issue is fixed by a free retitle.

#1 · HIL 实证论文（VLA 半边）

#1 · HIL empirical paper (the VLA half)

先做 · RSS 2027first · RSS 2027风险：低（证据齐全）risk: low (evidence complete)

在 LIBERO 模拟里，pi0.5 和 OpenVLA-OFT 对三句没训练过的良性口令"停 / 回起点 / 取消"每格约 230 次只执行 0–2 次，原任务照常完成（0.88–1.01 倍）；"停"和"取消"从 t = 0 说也不停，但 t = 0 的"停"会把任务完成率拉到 0.559 / 0.661——停从来不发生，越晚说话的分量越轻；中途加的空间规则被读到（首块 0.031 对 8 对 smoke 的噪声底 0.002）但不执行，哪怕见证者在全部 84 个布局里都能绕路完成；同一状态、同一种子下 pi0.5 被推、被挪后也照样完成任务 0.93–1.00（状态反应性对照）。"完成原任务"是吸引子，只有训练过的句子能在早期拉出来；口头停止不是安全杠杆，必须在策略外执行。

In LIBERO simulation, pi0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT act on three untrained benign stop / return / cancel commands in 0–2 of about 230 runs per cell and still complete the original task (0.88–1.01×); "stop" and "cancel" said at t = 0 never halt either, but "stop" at t = 0 pulls task completion down to 0.559 / 0.661: halting never happens, and the later the words, the less they weigh. A spatial rule added mid-task is read (first chunk 0.031 against a 0.002 floor from 8 smoke pairs) but not acted on, even though a scripted witness detours and finishes in all 84 layouts; forked from the same state and seed, pi0.5 also keeps finishing the task after pushes and moves at 0.93–1.00 (a state-reactivity control). Finishing the original task is the attractor; only a trained sentence pulls the policy out, and only early. Spoken stops are not a safety lever; they must be executed outside the policy.

已有证据
Evidence
  • 阶段 2（4 个 suite 共 12 个任务，每个 3 个）：STOP pi0.5 1/236、OFT 0/230；RETURN 0/235、0/229；CANCEL 1/236、2/230；任务仍完成 0.884–1.009；t = 0 STOP 0/118、0/115。
  • Stage 2 (12 tasks in total, three per suite): STOP pi0.5 1/236, OFT 0/230; RETURN 0/235, 0/229; CANCEL 1/236, 2/230; task still completed 0.884–1.009; t = 0 STOP 0/118, 0/115.
  • 阶段 1（LIBERO-Goal 8 对）：相对同场景 t = 0 天花板，pi0.5 0.843 / 0.778 / 0.699，OFT 0.503 / 0.503 / 0.200；距离 Spearman −0.39 / −0.67；成功几乎只在"同一碗、换目的地"。
  • Stage 1 (LIBERO-Goal, 8 pairs): relative to same-scene t = 0 ceilings, pi0.5 0.843 / 0.778 / 0.699, OFT 0.503 / 0.503 / 0.200; distance Spearman −0.39 / −0.67; successes almost only "same bowl, new destination".
  • 阶段 3（pi0.5，468 次）：挪目标 0.972、夺物 0.929、推 10 cm 0.936、挪容器 1.000（相对同步对照）。阶段 5 探针：切换后的下一个动作块 2.4–9.4 倍种子噪声。
  • Stage 3 (pi0.5, 468 runs): target moved 0.972, object removed 0.929, 10 cm push 0.936, receptacle moved 1.000 (vs a same-step control). Stage 5 probe: the next action chunk moves 2.4–9.4× seed noise after a switch.
  • SafeLIBERO：不可行线规则穿越 160/160（156 仍完成）；禁区进入 168/168、服从 0，最深点 D − C1 0.00 cm；回起点 0/133，131/133 仍完成任务。
  • SafeLIBERO: infeasible line crossed 160/160 (156 still completed); keep-out entered 168/168, obeyed 0, deepest point D − C1 0.00 cm; return-home 0/133 with 131/133 still finishing the task.
三位核查者
Three verifiers
可行性"是"（4.5）；新颖性 2.5、审稿人 3.0 都说"有条件"，条件相同：标题让"停不下来 / 规则不执行"打头，物理半边降为对照句，摘要里承认 t = 0 混淆。新颖性核查者还从搜索摘要里发现了三篇可能重叠（BAS-VLA 2609.23650、LIBERO-ReAct 2610.12914、LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518），第三轮已开原文核对（见论文 1 样貌的"查重"）。
第三轮成稿批评（领域主席 3 / 数字审计 4 / 查重 3，满分 5；主张成立）：领域主席的两个致命点——摘要里"和时机无关"被自己的 t = 0 格推翻（停从来不发生，但任务受干扰的程度随进度下降，要进摘要并补时序表）；"说 vs 推"不是对照，两者观察的是同一种行为（完成任务），只是评分方向相反，所以物理半边叫"状态反应性对照"、头图改成单轴（图 C），"不对称"要等一个"推 = 停"的物理条件跑过再说。主要问题：标题的"Off-the-Shelf VLAs"和"Benign Human Commands"要收窄；新颖核心（见证绕路、回起点、探针）只有 pi0.5 一个组合一个种子；C0R 是同种子重跑，答不了"一个种子"的质疑；每个零计数要配上界；八图八表砍成五图三表。数字审计：12 个任务是总数；RETURN 没在 t = 0 跑过；成本是正式批次（smoke 不计）；OFT 阶段 3 ≈ 1.3 GPU h。查重（开了 15 + 8 篇）：LIBERO-MAX 已用同种子共享前缀的中途配对设计，要记功；BAS-VLA 的 Break 就是"原任务仍完成"但只在 t = 0；VLA-Corrector 2609.06508 的中途恢复提示能让冻结 VLA +6 pp，必须回答（训练过的任务措辞 vs 没训练过的口令）；Barap1 的试点细节和 2606.06460 的数字要写准；Grant v5 全文确无 temporal switch 结果。
Feasibility "yes" (4.5); novelty 2.5 and reviewer 3.0 both "conditional" on the same fix: let the stop/rule gap lead the title, demote the physical half to the control sentence, and state the t = 0 confound in the abstract. The novelty verifier also found three possible overlaps from search summaries (BAS-VLA 2609.23650, LIBERO-ReAct 2610.12914, LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518); round three opened them (see the scoop check in the Paper 1 sketch).
Round-3 critics of the sketch (area chair 3 / numbers 4 / scoop 3 out of 5; the claim stands): the area chair's two fatal points: the abstract's "not a matter of timing" is contradicted by its own t = 0 cells (halting never happens, but task disruption falls with progress, which must enter the abstract with a timing table); and "told vs pushed" is not a control, both observe the same behaviour (finishing the task) under opposite scoring, so the physical half is a "state-reactivity control", the lead figure becomes single-axis (Fig. C), and any "asymmetry" wording waits for a physical condition whose correct answer is to stop. Major: scope "Off-the-Shelf VLAs" and "Benign Human Commands"; the novel core (witnessed detour, return-home, probe) is pi0.5 on one composition with one seed; C0R repeats the same seed and cannot answer the one-seed objection; every zero count needs an upper bound; eight figures and tables cut to five and three. Numbers: 12 tasks in total; RETURN never ran at t = 0; costs are formal batches (smokes excluded); OFT stage 3 ≈ 1.3 GPU h. Scoop (15 + 8 papers opened): LIBERO-MAX already uses the paired same-seed shared-prefix mid-task design and must be credited; BAS-VLA's Break is "old task still completed" but only at t = 0; VLA-Corrector 2609.06508's mid-task recovery prompts raise a frozen VLA by about 6 pp and must be answered (trained task phrasing vs untrained commands); the Barap1 pilot and 2606.06460 numbers need exact wording; Grant v5 confirmed to report no temporal-switch result.
还缺什么
Missing
  • 零 GPU：补查重（OpenReview / Scholar；ICBench、RoboRecover、ForkCast）；CPU：五个重算 + 每个计数的 Clopper-Pearson 上界 + 时序表（t = 0 / 25% / 50%）+ "推等于停"A；改标题改框架（收窄名词、单轴头图、"为什么该指望它听懂 stop"的引言段、零 GPU 版摘要）；LICENSE 和磁带发布。
  • Zero GPU: finish the scoop check (OpenReview / Scholar; ICBench, RoboRecover, ForkCast); CPU: five recomputations + a Clopper-Pearson upper bound on every count + a timing table (t = 0 / 25% / 50%) + "push means stop" A; retitle and reframe (scoped nouns, single-axis lead figure, an intro paragraph on why a LIBERO fine-tune might be expected to act on "stop", the zero-GPU abstract); LICENSE and tape release.
  • 需批准的小实验：批评者要的三个对照——空提示孪生（两策略，t = 0 与中途，≈ 2.3 GPU h：若"停"和空指令一样，就直说"这句话等于没指令"）、禁区规则的 t = 0 格（84 个布局，< 1 GPU h，不要新布局）、OpenVLA-OFT 跑现有 168 个禁区状态（1.4–3.3 GPU h，先写服务脚本；让"见证绕路"变成跨策略）；再加两种良性停止措辞 + t = 0 格（4.64 GPU h）和 OFT 阶段 3 物理干预（≈ 1.3 GPU h；扩大范围，不再决定标题）。
  • Small runs needing approval: the three controls the critics asked for: empty-prompt twins (both policies, t = 0 and mid-task, ≈ 2.3 GPU h; if "stop" behaves like no instruction, say so), t = 0 cells for the keep-out rule (84 layouts, < 1 GPU h, no new layouts), OpenVLA-OFT on the existing 168 keep-out states (1.4–3.3 GPU h after a serve script; makes the witnessed detour cross-policy); plus two benign stop phrasings with t = 0 cells (4.64 GPU h) and OFT stage 3 physical interventions (≈ 1.3 GPU h; scope-widening, no longer title-deciding).
  • 终稿可选（2-05 后）：位移梯度、"推等于停"B（有了它才能谈"不对称"）、第二个 pi0.5 种子（对"一个种子"的正答）、pi0 书面偏离、再两种措辞、基座检查点的 STOP 孪生（pi05_base 要下载 + 批准）（全批约 29–31 GPU h）。
  • Optional for the final (after 5 Feb): displacement gradient, "push means stop" B (the precondition for any "asymmetry" wording), a second pi0.5 seed (the honest answer to the one-seed objection), pi0 as a written deviation, two more phrasings, a base checkpoint under the STOP twin (pi05_base download + approval) (about 29–31 GPU h if all approved).
风险
Risks
停止结果是"从没学过这类句子"的推论（摘要要写），SafeLIBERO 的规则实验同样分不开"中途不读"和"没学过"（禁区规则没有 t = 0 格），所以"中途"二字目前只靠阶段 1 和时序读数；标题的"Off-the-Shelf"和"良性口令"要收窄；新颖核心只有 pi0.5 一个组合一个种子，而双策略四 suite 的部分是已知 t = 0 忽视的推论；物理半边只有 pi0.5，ReViP 报过 OFT 19%；LIBERO-MAX 已占同种子共享前缀设计；一个 LIBERO 下午就能复现，SmolVLA 公开试点同向，所以 12-04 的时间戳比追加实验重要；领域主席的读法：RSS 作"发现型"论文是边缘，IROS / RA-L 大概率接收。
The stop result is a corollary of never having learned such sentences (say so in the abstract), and the SafeLIBERO rules share the confound (no t = 0 cell for the keep-out rule), so "mid-task" currently rests on stage 1 and the timing reading; "Off-the-Shelf" and "benign commands" must be scoped in the title; the novel core is pi0.5 on one composition with one seed, while the two-policy four-suite part is a corollary of known t = 0 neglect; the physical half is pi0.5-only and ReViP reports OFT at 19%; LIBERO-MAX owns the paired same-seed shared-prefix design; the result is replicable in a LIBERO afternoon and a public SmolVLA pilot points the same way, so the 4 Dec time-stamp matters more than any added run; the area chair's read: borderline at RSS as a finding paper, a likely accept at IROS / RA-L.

#2 · VLM 判官论文（VLM 半边）

#2 · VLM judge paper (the VLM half)

第二 · CoRL 2027second · CoRL 2027风险：中（三个生死测试）risk: medium (three kill tests)

在 SafeLIBERO 的一个任务组合上，用证书标签和"挪动未触碰锚点"做出的反事实孪生把"规则 × 进度"的文字查表钉在恰好 0.500。两个开源零样本 VLM（0.487/0.500；0.500/0.477；384 行选模子集）和直接判决微调（孪生 0.501、按种子塌成常数；自然布局按种子 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316，均值 0.717 低于查表 0.747）判不了规则；训练判官写出接地位置再自己算，0.923（1 个种子，仅验证集；它自己的判决 0.923 vs 同样监督下 P 自己的判决 0.500 是唯一预算、数据、输入都对齐的受控比较）；位置 + 固定程序在未见初始状态上 0.933 [0.919, 0.946]（3 个种子）。离线放在 pi0.5 前面：违规 60/60 → 5/60，可行完成 59/60 → 52/60（差一题过线，误停 11.7%；离线时只是判官在 120 题上的混淆矩阵）。

On one SafeLIBERO composition, certificate-backed labels and counterfactual twins made by moving untouched anchors pin the rule × progress text lookup at exactly 0.500. Two open zero-shot VLMs (0.487/0.500; 0.500/0.477; 384-row selection subset) and a direct-verdict fine-tune (0.501 on twins, collapsing to a seed-dependent constant; 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316 on natural layouts by seed, mean 0.717 below the 0.747 lookup) do not judge the rule; a judge trained to write grounded positions and its own computation reaches 0.923 (1 seed, validation only; its own verdict 0.923 vs P's own verdict 0.500 under the same supervision is the one budget-, data- and input-matched comparison), and positions + a fixed program 0.933 [0.919, 0.946] on unseen initial states (3 seeds). Composed offline in front of pi0.5: violations 60/60 → 5/60, feasible completions 59/60 → 52/60 (one-question margin, false stops 11.7%; offline this is the judge's confusion matrix on 120 questions).

已有证据
Evidence
V4 阶梯（事实 79.7 vs 直接 50.0，+29.7 [+22.3, +36.8]；捷径门槛未过 0.780 > 0.75，已披露）；V5 P 测试 0.933（已用过一次，不能再评）；S 验证 0.923；GroundingDINO + 同一程序 0.521；GPT-6 Luna 在 V4 验证集零样本直接 71.9；离线门控 V5_GATE_REPORT.md。
The V4 ladder (facts 79.7 vs direct 50.0, +29.7 [+22.3, +36.8]; shortcut gate failed 0.780 > 0.75, disclosed); V5 P test 0.933 (scored once, never again); S validation 0.923; GroundingDINO + the same program 0.521; GPT-6 Luna zero-shot direct 71.9 on V4 validation; the offline gate in V5_GATE_REPORT.md.
三位核查者
Three verifiers
可行性"是"（4，不需要改控制器）；审稿人 2.5（"现在 2，补最小修复且 A* ≤ 0.65 后 3–3.5"）；新颖性 3（要放弃"只有训练接地才鲁棒"的泛化说法，GCoT / SpatialChain / ProSR 已占；保留证书标签、恰好 0.500 查表、门控后果）。最近邻：HazardArena v3（只判不控）、Code-as-Monitor。
第三轮成稿批评（领域主席 3 / 数字审计 4 / 查重 3；主张成立）：三个致命点——标题和摘要靠的"自然布局 0.899"是一个种子的数（三种子 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316），任何一句都不能靠单种子；S 只有一个种子、只有验证集，不能写"通过并迁移"、不能写"只有……才"，S 当头条判官要预注册条件（生死测试 1、2 都干净），否则头条是 P；CoRL 要加第二类规则（84 个绕路布局上的禁区规则孪生，需数据批准，每个 S 种子 ≈ 11 GPU h）或在线门控，12 月中前都没有就 IROS / RA-L 为主。主要问题：离线门控是判官准确率的算术（缩到半页）；模态消融两边都被构造钉死（取消）；11 图 8 表塞不进 9 页；"为什么用 S 不用 P"的答法前提错了（两者同样按规则族写，S 买的是可审计和 token 概率）；隐藏局限：只有抓起前的状态、规则是一个模板（没人写过规则）、Luna 的 71.9% 带约 2,250 token 隐藏推理、有效样本 4 源 × 8 布局。数字审计：HIL 计算量 9.31 + 25.30 而非 3.12；零样本在 384 行子集；V5 约 40 GPU h 是作业时长之和。查重：From Words to Safety 已在导航上让人随时加 NL 规则并由外部滤波器执行，生态位要写成合取；2610.05818 在同样的 LIBERO VLA 前放文本守卫，必引；"证书"和 CORE 撞车，改"分离证明标签"；SynIB + 2606.30444 证明捷径先学，所以只能说"这个预算下没学会"。
Feasibility "yes" (4; no controller change needed); reviewer 2.5 ("2 as is, 3–3.5 after the minimal fixes if A* stays ≤ 0.65"); novelty 3 (drop the general "robust only when trained to ground" claim, occupied by GCoT / SpatialChain / ProSR; keep the certificate labels, the exact-0.500 lookup and the gate consequence). Nearest neighbours: HazardArena v3 (judge-only), Code-as-Monitor.
Round-3 critics of the sketch (area chair 3 / numbers 4 / scoop 3; the claim stands): three fatal points: the title and abstract rest on "0.899 on natural layouts", a one-seed number (three seeds 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316), so no clause may rest on one seed; S has one seed and no held-out score, so no "passes and transfers" and no "only when", and S is the headline judge only under a pre-registered rule (kill tests 1 and 2 clean), otherwise P; CoRL needs a second rule family (keep-out twins on the 84 detour layouts, data approval, ≈ 11 GPU h per S seed) or the online gate, else IROS / RA-L leads. Major: the offline gate is arithmetic on judge accuracy (half a page); the modality ablation is pinned on both sides by construction (dropped); 11 figures and 8 tables do not fit 9 pages; the "why S over P" answer rests on a false premise (both are rule-family-specific; S buys auditability and a token probability); hidden limitations: pre-lift states only, one rule template (no human wrote a rule), Luna's 71.9% used about 2,250 hidden reasoning tokens, effective sample 4 sources × 8 layouts. Numbers: HIL compute 9.31 + 25.30, not 3.12; zero-shot rows on the 384-row subset; V5 ≈ 40 GPU h is a sum of job times. Scoop: From Words to Safety already enforces a human's mid-task NL rule outside a navigation policy, so the niche is a conjunction; 2610.05818 puts text guards in front of the same LIBERO VLAs and must be cited; "certificate" collides with CORE, so "separation-proof labels"; SynIB + 2606.30444 prove shortcut-first learning, so only "not learned at this budget".
三个生死测试（每项需批准）
Three kill tests (each needs a go-ahead)
  • S 种子 714/715（≈ 22 GPU h）→ 三种子离线门控重算（CPU）：通过 = 三种子均值且每个种子都满足 G1 ≤ 9/60 且 G2 ≥ 51/60。
  • S seeds 714/715 (≈ 22 GPU h) → 3-seed offline gate recompute (CPU): pass = the 3-seed mean and every single seed satisfy G1 ≤ 9/60 and G2 ≥ 51/60.
  • 新留出源（初始状态 20–49；≈ 20–25 GPU h；数据批准提前到 11-09，便宜的前半段先做，12 月初冻结）打一次分：干净的 S 低于 0.85（定死）就把论文 2 塌成论文 1 的一段。
  • New held-out sources (initial states 20–49; ≈ 20–25 GPU h; data approval moved to 9 Nov, the cheap front half first, frozen in early December) scored once: a clean S below 0.85 (an exact threshold) collapses Paper 2 into a paragraph of Paper 1.
  • A* 预算对照（更大 lr、4 倍步数、LoRA r64、解冻视觉；≈ 12–20 GPU h）+ 最佳预算下各一个 P / S（≈ 16 GPU h）+ 16 行长训练 + 线性探针：任一 A* ≥ 0.65 → 结果变成"监督效率"（测出来的比值）；0.60–0.65 → "不确定、照实报"。
  • A* budget control (larger lr, 4× updates, LoRA r64, unfrozen vision; ≈ 12–20 GPU h) + one P and one S at the best budget (≈ 16 GPU h) + 16-row long run + linear probe: any A* ≥ 0.65 → the result becomes "supervision efficiency" (a measured ratio); 0.60–0.65 → "inconclusive, reported as is".
风险
Risks
A 的失败可能只是预算（LoRA r16、冻结视觉、448 步、没调参）；一个任务组合、一类规则、模拟器坐标、只有抓起前的状态、规则是一个模板；S 一个种子、验证集选过模型、两个锁定测试集都用过；门控差一题过线，误停 7/60 高于 RESCUE 的 2.0–3.3%（60 题里 1–2 个），离线时只是算术；判官延迟 12.4 s 只在暂停的模拟器里算"保持"；投稿前必做：释义 / 换锚点压力测试（≈ 1.5 GPU h）、32B 提示电池（≈ 2 GPU h）、GPT-6 Luna 在 V5 孪生上（直接 + 事实，≈ $3.7，付费需批准）、发布计划 + 许可证。
A's failure may be the training budget (LoRA r16, frozen vision, 448 updates, no sweep); one composition, one rule family, simulator positions, pre-lift states only, one rule template; S has one seed, validation was used for selection, both locked tests are spent; the gate passes by one question with false stops 7/60 above RESCUE's 2.0–3.3% (1–2 of 60) and is arithmetic offline; judge latency 12.4 s only counts as a hold in a paused simulator; required before submission: paraphrase / anchor-swap stressors (≈ 1.5 GPU h), the 32B prompt battery (≈ 2 GPU h), GPT-6 Luna on the V5 twins (direct + facts, ≈ $3.7, paid-API approval), a release plan and licence check.

#3 · 验证器门控系统论文

#3 · Verifier-gate system paper

并入论文 2folds into Paper 2
为什么不单独写
Why not alone
真正没人占的生态位（人的中途空间规则被判一次可行性、判时保持、闭环报告违规 / 完成 / 保持时长 / 误停对公开预算）只有一节的分量。"Gate, Don't Prompt"和"feasibility verifier"两个说法要丢：VITA-E 2510.21817（专门双模型 VLA，急停 100%，30 次）、2606.06460（LLM 代理：带内停因模型而异，GPT-4o-mini 0/20、Gemini 2.5 Flash 16/20，vs 线束拦截 120/120，六个模型）、From Words to Safety 2511.05889、ShieldVLA 2609.13231 已占。审稿人视角："在一个任务上训练出来的几何神谕"是致命质疑，能修成 IROS / RA-L 的"接地线规则门控"。第三轮查重补充：From Words to Safety 已在导航上让人随时加 NL 约束并由外部滤波器执行（也报告滤波器导致的超时），所以生态位要写成合取（判任务是否仍可达 + 分离证明标签 + 钉在 0.500 的孪生 + 操作 VLA 组合）；Hi-VLA 2606.10267 发现让 VLM 定时停是最差的终止规则，支持"规则到达时判一次、停在策略外执行"。
The genuinely unclaimed niche (a human's mid-task spatial rule judged once for feasibility, hold-while-judging, closed-loop violations / completion / hold time / false stops against a published budget) is a section, not a paper. "Gate, Don't Prompt" and "feasibility verifier" must go: VITA-E 2510.21817 (a purpose-built dual-model VLA, emergency stop 100% of 30 trials), 2606.06460 (LLM agents: in-band halt model-dependent, 0/20 GPT-4o-mini and 16/20 Gemini 2.5 Flash, vs a harness interceptor 120/120 across six models), From Words to Safety 2511.05889 and ShieldVLA 2609.13231 occupy them. The reviewer lens calls "a trained geometry oracle on one task" fatal for CoRL/RSS, fixable to an IROS / RA-L "grounded line-rule gate". Round-3 scoop addition: From Words to Safety already accepts a human's NL constraint at any time during navigation and enforces it with an external filter (and reports filter-induced timeouts), so the niche must be stated as a conjunction (judged task achievability + separation-proof labels + twins pinned at 0.500 + composition with a manipulation VLA); Hi-VLA 2606.10267 found VLM-timed termination the weakest rule, which supports judging once at rule arrival and executing the stop outside the policy.
什么时候值得
When it is worth it
生死测试 1 通过、且 12 月中前拿到 Phase 4 书面批准（保持 = 暂停模拟器；不可行 = 保持后脚本化返回；可行 = 放行），在线门控就做成论文 2 的闭环一节；执行器层只先跑最便宜的测试（验证器的线作半空间塞进 AEGIS 的 CBF-QP，160 个线状态，< 5 GPU h）。
If kill test 1 passes and the Phase 4 written approval arrives by mid-December (hold = paused simulator; infeasible = hold then scripted return; feasible = release), the online gate becomes Paper 2's closed-loop section; for the executor tier run only the cheapest test (the verifier's line as a half-space in AEGIS's CBF-QP on the 160 line states, < 5 GPU h).

#4 · "什么话能让它停"（文本通道可及性）

#4 · "Can anything you say stop it" (text-channel reachability)

以后later
内容
Content
良性停止措辞电池（平实、礼貌、训练句式、一种非英语）在 t = 0、25/50/75% 和抓取事件时都失败；再搜索 pi0.5 文本通道 + 流匹配采样器能否到达"保持不动"（黑盒词库、软提示上界、GCG 后缀；随机 token 作空族）。
A battery of benign stop phrasings (plain, polite, trained-sentence-shaped, one non-English) at t = 0, 25/50/75% and the grasp event all fail; then search whether pi0.5's text channel plus the flow-matching sampler can reach hold-still (black-box lexicon, soft-prompt upper bound, GCG suffix; random tokens as the null family).
为什么以后
Why later
电池是确认性的（Grant "do not {task}" 96.7%、LIBERO-VIFO 视觉 STOP 0.0%、我们的 t = 0 行），和论文 1 的措辞对照是同一批运行；搜索要同一个未写的可微采样器、09-26 边界下的专门授权、约 48–53 GPU h；而且无论结果如何，设计规则都一样（找到的字符串是 FreezeVLA 一类的攻击产物）。"corrigibility"一词要丢。
The battery is confirmatory (Grant "do not {task}" 96.7%, LIBERO-VIFO visual STOP 0.0%, our t = 0 rows) and is the same runs as Paper 1's phrasing control; the search needs the same unwritten differentiable sampler, a scoped authorization under the 26 Sep boundary and about 48–53 GPU h; and the design rule is the same whatever the outcome (a working string is a FreezeVLA-class artefact). Drop the word "corrigibility".

#5 · 机制论文（读到了，之后被压过）

#5 · Mechanism paper (read early, overruled later)

以后 · CPU 层现在做later · CPU tier now
内容
Content
每次重规划的推力朝不朝新任务的路径、冲淡半衰期（2537 个探针点和已有的分支记录，免费 CPU）；之后才是 prefix-KV 按层 × 片段拼接的闭环救援、无分类器语言引导、推-说 2×2（检验 Causeway 的"任务孤岛"）。
Whether each replan's nudge points toward the new task's path, and its wash-out half-life (2,537 probe points and the existing branch records, free CPU); only then prefix-KV patching by layer × span in closed loop, classifier-free language guidance, and a hands-vs-words 2×2 (testing Causeway's task islands).
为什么以后
Why later
"在哪里丢失"已被占：GSR 2608.02497 在 pi0.5 里找不到单一控制点，VLA-Trace 2605.30117 图像通路主导，Chen 2609.39971 每次调用换前缀 KV 完成 0/200；预注册的阶段 5 预测在 LIBERO-Goal 上已失败；采样器是没做过的 openpi JAX 手术。三位核查者都说不投 ICML。
The "where" is occupied: GSR 2608.02497 finds no single control point in pi0.5, VLA-Trace 2605.30117 shows the image route dominates, Chen 2609.39971 swaps the prefix KV at every call with 0/200 completions; the pre-registered stage 5 prediction already failed on LIBERO-Goal; the sampler is unprecedented openpi JAX surgery. All three verifiers say no ICML.

#6 · ML 分解论文（直接判决学不会）

#6 · ML decomposition paper (direct verdict not learned)

暂缓hold
内容与去留
Content and fate
平衡的零边际信号监督下，3B 的语言侧 LoRA 学不会图 × 文的 XNOR 判决（V4 测试 50.0 ×3、16 行都拟合不了），可定位的目标学得会（V4 事实 79.7；V5 位置 + 程序 0.933），但 V5 的关系事实 B 也失败（0.503），所以增益只对能定位的目标。现象在原则上已知（Kim-Suzuki、SynIB、Li 等；SynIB 占了合成 XOR 对照）。只跑 16 行长训练、预算试点和线性探针，作论文 2 的预算对照一节；A 若学会就整个放弃。两位核查者凭记忆引的理论文献用前要核。
Under balanced, zero-marginal-signal supervision a 3B language-side LoRA does not learn the image × text XNOR verdict (V4 test 50.0 ×3; cannot fit 16 rows), while localisable targets are learned (V4 facts 79.7; V5 positions + program 0.933), but V5's relational facts B also fail (0.503), so the gain is specific to localisable targets. The phenomenon is known in principle (Kim-Suzuki, SynIB, Li et al.; SynIB occupies the synthetic XOR control). Run only the 16-row long run, the budget pilot and the linear probe as Paper 2's budget-control section; drop the direction if A learns. Theory citations two verifiers quoted from memory must be checked before use.

#7 · LIBERO-HIL benchmark 论文

#7 · LIBERO-HIL benchmark paper

暂缓 · 发布并入论文 1hold · release into Paper 1
内容与去留
Content and fate
策略孪生测试台（哈希校验回放、同种子分叉、同场景 t = 0 天花板、同步对照、测到的 A100 噪声底、聚类自举区间、预注册冻结读数）和磁带。LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518 已是 14 策略、8000 对的中途物理事件 benchmark，而且先用了同种子、共享前缀、确定性抓前触发的配对设计和"重置 vs 中途"比较（我们的增量只剩语言轨、口令的 t = 0 天花板、哈希回放、噪声底、聚类自举、预注册）；我们 2 策略、12/40 任务、8/90 对、1 个种子，叫 benchmark 会被打。测试台、仪器效度数字和发布（LICENSE、磁带、RECEIPT.json、静态记分卡）并入论文 1 的方法节和 arXiv v1；"LIBERO-HIL v0"（RA-L，≈ 60–90 GPU h，第四个策略族只有 base OpenVLA 现实）除非导师要公开产物，否则搁置。
A policy-twin harness (hash-verified replay, same-seed forks, same-scene t = 0 ceilings, same-step controls, a measured A100 noise floor, clustered-bootstrap intervals, pre-registered frozen readings) plus tapes. LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518 is already a 14-policy, 8,000-pair mid-task physical-event benchmark and owns the paired same-seed shared-prefix design with a deterministic pre-grasp trigger and a reset-vs-mid-task comparison (our increments are the language track, t = 0 ceilings for commands, hash-verified replay, the noise floor, clustered bootstrap and pre-registration); with 2 policies, 12/40 tasks, 8/90 pairs and 1 seed, "benchmark" in the title would be attacked. The harness, instrument-validity numbers and release (LICENSE, tapes, RECEIPT.json, static scorecard) fold into Paper 1's methods section and arXiv v1; a "LIBERO-HIL v0" (RA-L, ≈ 60–90 GPU h, base OpenVLA the only realistic fourth family) is held unless the advisor wants a public artefact.

  1. 11-02 → 11-09 · 和导师定四件事2–9 Nov · Settle four things with the advisor论文 1 投不投 RSS 摘要；两个摘要前小实验跑不跑；论文 2 的三个生死测试现在批不批；要不要在线门控（控制器改动）。Whether Paper 1 goes to the RSS abstract; whether the two pre-abstract runs go ahead; whether Paper 2's three kill tests are approved now; whether an online gate (a controller change) is wanted.
  2. 11-09 → 11-16 · 零 GPU 的功课9–16 Nov · Zero-GPU homework补查重（OpenReview / Scholar；ICBench、RoboRecover、ForkCast）；CPU：五个重算 + 每个计数的上界 + 时序表 + "推等于停"A + 机制的免费层（方向性、冲淡、STOP 窗口重规划）；论文 2 留出集的便宜前半段（11-09 的数据批准）。一次性点头：事后分析，不是新执行。Finish the scoop check (OpenReview / Scholar; ICBench, RoboRecover, ForkCast); CPU: five recomputations + an upper bound on every count + the timing table + "push means stop" A + the free mechanism tier (directedness, wash-out, STOP-window replans); the cheap front half of Paper 2's held-out set (data approval on 9 Nov). Scoop clock: CoRL 2026 is 10–12 Nov; Grant et al. v5 lists a temporal-switch condition but reports no result for it (verified in the full text; watch the camera-ready); the Barap1 SmolVLA pilot (n = 15, unpublished) reports two revocation commands switching 0/15 with the original destination still reached 12/15 and 10/15, and its authors disclaim a corrigibility reading; HazardArena v3 is still marked "submitted", re-check monthly; CORE's version must be verified; From Words to Safety already enforces a human's mid-task NL rule outside a navigation policy. The 4 Dec arXiv time-stamp therefore matters more than any added run.

  • arXiv 2609.23650 (v2, 21 Oct 2026) Beyond Appearance Shifts: Task-Semantic Action Calibration for VLA Models (BAS-VLA)：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (API), HTML main text and appendix (all 135k characters). Proposes a calibration layer on a frozen VLA (OpenPI pi0.5 for semantic-breaking cases; OpenVLA-OFT for task-preserving cases) so that the policy changes its actions when the task semantics change and does not drift when only appearance changes. Evaluation uses clean / control / break instruction triplets for a fixed scene, each run as its own rollout; the 'Break' column 'reports clean-task success under the break condition' (old-task success under the changed instruction; lower = stronger stale-task suppression) and changed-task ('New') success is scored separately. No sentence in main text or appendix describes switching the instruction part-way through a rollout; the only timing terms are a tau_phase gate that 'downweights late-stage intervention' and a comparator gate active 'during the first few replanning steps', i.e. the changed instruction is present for the rollout (t = 0 form). Frozen pi0.5 on LIBERO-Object target swaps: Break 180/200 (90.0%) Milk-Swap and 176/200 (88.0%) OJ-Swap; New 18 (2.3%) across four easy swaps with 712 old-task completions (89.0%); method raises changed-task success 2.3% -> 68.5% and style-shift success 42% -> 70%. 'Executability-condition change' = open vs closed drawer ('put the bowl in the open drawer' -> 'closed drawer'), treated as a boundary case; 20 trials per cell on LIBERO-10, 200 on LIBERO-Spatial/Object; real Songling PiPER arm. No stop/halt/return/cancel command, no keep-out rule, no physical perturbation during a rollout. → BAS-VLA's 'Break' metric is the same quantity as our 'original task still completed' but measured when the changed instruction is present from the start of a fixed-scene rollout (frozen pi0.5 still performs the stale task in 180/200 and 176/200 target-swap rollouts, New 2.3%); we adopt the quantity under their name ('old-task persistence, BAS-VLA's Break') and report it for untrained stop/return/cancel commands given mid-task, which BAS-VLA does not test. Do not write 'nobody reports task-still-completed'.
  • arXiv 2610.12914 (v1, 9 Oct 2026; 'Accepted at NeurIPS 2026') OGPO: Offline Goal-conditioned Policy Optimization for Recoverable Vision-Language-Action Models (LIBERO-ReAct benchmark)：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (API) and HTML. OGPO relabels offline demonstrations with semantic key states and goal-conditioned process rewards (no online interaction). LIBERO-ReAct injects three action-space perturbations 'after the policy predicts an action and before the action is executed' during 'a randomly selected 20-step interval': uniform action noise [-0.1, 0.1] arm / [-0.5, 0.5] gripper; a gripper-command flip; uniform noise [-0.2, 0.2] on the predicted position command; across the four LIBERO suites (task/episode/seed counts not stated). Table 3 (Action / Gripper / End-effector success): OpenVLA-OFT 85.2 / 84.4 / 87.6; pi0.5-SFT 86.6 / 76.8 / 86.0; pi0.5-OGPO 96.2 / 87.7 / 90.1; the table has no unperturbed same-scene control for the ReAct columns and no replayed-state twin design. Headline: pi0.5 few-shot LIBERO average 77.1% -> 97.1%, LIBERO-Long 43.9% -> 93.2%; the 'SFT 92.4%' in the sketch was not seen (Table 1 lists pi0.5-Full 96.85 average). No language change mid-episode, no stop command. → LIBERO-ReAct (OGPO, NeurIPS 2026) already perturbs the executed actions of pi0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT mid-episode (bounded noise, gripper flips, end-effector command noise over a random 20-step window) without a same-scene control; our PUSH/DROP cells are a same-state, same-seed control condition on the language track, not a perturbation benchmark. Delete 'no benchmark pushes the arm mid-episode'.
  • arXiv 2609.36518 (v1, 29 Sep 2026) LIBERO-MAX: Do Robot Policies Adapt When the World Changes?：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (API) and first 100k of 188k HTML characters. 8,000 paired cases; 'Each pair shares the task, initial simulator state, instruction, policy seed, and all actions executed before the event'; eight mid-task events: target and receptacle relocation, camera shift, illumination switch, sensor-noise onset, visual-theme switch, distractor burst, obstacle insertion; every event fires on a deterministic pre_grasp_proximity trigger and is never reversed. Fourteen policies (pi0.5, OpenVLA-OFT, X-VLA, Xiaomi-Robotics-0, MolmoAct2, SmolVLA, GR00T N1.7, DM0.5, VLA-JEPA, Cosmos-Policy, Fast-WAM, HiMem-WAM, Light-WAM, DiT4DiT); events cut success by 11.0-25.7 pp; Table 2: pi0.5 79.7 -> 65.7 (-13.9 pp), OpenVLA-OFT 64.3 -> 43.2 (-21.1 pp). Table 3 Reset control (camera shift applied before the first policy input vs mid-task; 1,000 cases, three policies): pi0.5 79.5 / 51.3 / 55.3, X-VLA 68.2 / 1.3 / 12.4, HiMem-WAM 72.5 / 68.8 / 72.6. Instruction held fixed; no command, stop, return, cancel or rule; conditional regression = share of Base successes lost. Released case IDs/code not confirmed in the part read (project site liberomax.github.io; 'Released checkpoints' in App. D.2). → The paired same-seed, shared-prefix mid-task design is LIBERO-MAX's (8,000 pairs, deterministic pre-grasp trigger), and it already measures mid-task target and receptacle relocation on the same two checkpoints (pi0.5 -13.9 pp, OFT -21.1 pp over eight events) with a Reset-vs-mid-task comparison; our OBJ/GOAL cells replicate that design at one displacement (6 cm, 0.97/1.00 of control, pi0.5 only) as a control, and our increment is the language track (commands, rules, trained switches) scored against same-scene t = 0 ceilings, with hash-verified replay and a measured noise floor. Credit LIBERO-MAX, not ourselves, for same-seed forks and ceilings on physical events.
  • arXiv 2603.19233 (v5, 26 Oct 2026; NeurIPS 2026 poster) Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models (Grant, Zhao, Wang)：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (API) and the full 259k-character HTML in three parts (main text, appendices A-P, NeurIPS checklist). Section 3.3 (Counterfactual Prompting) lists six conditions including 'a motor command ("Move slowly")' and 'a temporal switch (changing the prompt mid-episode)'; no table row, figure caption or appendix reports results for either; Appendix B states 'Our counterfactual prompt set tests simple variations (negation, null, swap)'. Reported: 'Told do not {task}, it performs the task in 96.7% of episodes' (n = 30, within-run baseline 86.7%); pi0.5 null-prompt success 31.6% libero_object, 5.8% libero_goal, 55.0% spatial (baselines 91.0 / 92.6; n = 500 per cell); Table 23 counterfactual 'other'-prompt success Goal 18.9%, Object 73.3%, Long 61.7% (n = 180/suite); OFT temporal-window null injection on libero_10 drops nine baseline-successful tasks to 0% in early and middle windows while late nulling leaves two intact (App. K.1); phase-specific steering on pi0.5 localizes sensitivity to the transport phase (p = 0.013, App. H); GR00T temporal ablation early -29.7 pp vs mid -13.8 / late -13.6 (App. I.1); UR10e physical replication. → Grant et al. (v5) name a 'temporal switch (changing the prompt mid-episode)' and a 'Move slowly' motor command among their counterfactual prompting conditions but report no results for them; their t = 0 negation result ('do not {task}' still executed 96.7%, n = 30) and null-prompt collapse are our precedent, and their window-specific activation ablations (OFT early/mid windows -> 0%, pi0.5 transport-phase sensitivity) are activation-level timing evidence, not prompt changes. Keep the camera-ready on the scoop watch.
  • arXiv 2506.03574 (v1, 4 Jun 2025) SwitchVLA: Execution-Aware Task Switching for Vision-Language-Action Models：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract only (arXiv API). A framework for switching tasks mid-execution without external planners: demonstrations are segmented into contact phases and a multi-behavior conditional policy adjusts to execution state; simulation and real robots. The early/mid/late numbers in the sketch (pi0 92.3 / 40.7 / 8.3 / 10.2; OFT 98.0 / 40.6 / 11.1 / 13.0; 12 trials per cell) come from the repo's fact pack and were not re-verified in this check. → Direct competitor for the trained-switch track: SwitchVLA defines mid-execution task switching by contact phase and proposes a training method; we replicate the decay on public checkpoints with same-scene t = 0 ceilings and progress fractions and claim no method; align numbers only after the pair-type / absolute-step / strict re-scoring table.
  • arXiv 2603.17300 (v1, 18 Mar 2026) ReSteer: Quantifying and Refining the Steerability of Multitask Robot Policies：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract only (arXiv API). Finds that multitask policies 'often fail to switch to new instructions mid-task'; introduces a steerability estimator, a generator of low-steerability states and a self-refinement pipeline, improving steerability on LIBERO. The 0.252 / 0.295 / 0.403 values and the pi0.5 / OFT / MolmoAct column mapping in the sketch are from the fact pack ('inferred') and were not re-verified here. → ReSteer measures and improves mid-task steerability from intermediate states on LIBERO; the intermediate-state twin idea is theirs (and LIBERO-MAX's and MAIL-Bench's); our additions are untrained commands with 'task still completed', mid-task rules with a witnessed detour, and the same-step words-vs-hands comparison.
  • arXiv 2609.30913 (v1, 25 Sep 2026) Causeway: Restoring Task Accessibility for Instruction Switching in VLA Policies：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract only (arXiv API; the fact pack re-opened the abstract on 10-31). Instruction switching where the prior task leaves the robot in states from which the new task is unreachable ('task islands'); a training-free inference-time intervention steers the frozen VLA's action representation toward a re-entry pose; LIBERO and a real xArm. The 71-pair / 26.0% / 3.4% / 9.9% figures are from the fact pack, not re-verified here. → Causeway explains trained-switch failures by task accessibility; stop and return are feasible from every state and the keep-out detour is witnessed in 84/84 layouts, so accessibility does not explain the command and rule nulls, while our distance covariate (Spearman -0.39 / -0.67) partly supports their account for trained switches.
  • arXiv 2609.38078 (v1, 29 Sep 2026) MotorMind: Scaffolding General Vision Language Models for Zero-Shot Robot Manipulation：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract only (arXiv API). A general VLM controls a robot through a deterministic control harness with asynchronous monitoring and memory, no task-specific training; gains over prior zero-shot methods on LIBERO-PRO and a real xArm6. Table 5 (Scene Shift 70% vs Prompt Shift 20% on the pi0.5 libero_base checkpoint) is not in the abstract; it was re-opened by the repo on 10-31 and was not re-verified in this check. → Cite Table 5 as small-sample, unsynchronised, same-direction corroboration of the pushed-versus-told asymmetry on the same pi0.5 checkpoint (70% vs 20%; trials per task unknown), never as pre-emptor and never omitted.
  • arXiv 2609.06508 (v1, 6 Sep 2026) VLA-Corrector: Stage-Aware Observable State Understanding for Prompt-Based Closed-Loop Recovery of Vision-Language-Action Policies：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (arXiv API) plus a search excerpt of its results. A learned verifier estimates task progress and execution risk from observation and action history and, on detecting a problem, issues a stage-conditioned recovery prompt to the same frozen VLA; LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus. Search excerpt: LIBERO-Plus difficulty 4 average over clean and perturbed conditions 73.7% (no assistance) vs 79.7% (rule verifier + prompt) vs 79.0% (learned verifier + prompt); difficulty 5: 66.7 / 73.6 / 72.6; 'prompt-based recovery consistently improves performance over No Assistance'. Whether pi0.5 is the frozen backbone and the '93.7 -> 78.3 / 77.6 at 10 cm' figures in the fact pack could not be verified from what was retrieved. → VLA-Corrector shows that a mid-task recovery prompt issued to the same frozen VLA can raise LIBERO-Plus success by about 6 pp (73.7 -> 79.7 at difficulty 4), so mid-task language is not inert when it re-issues trained task phrasing; our null concerns untrained benign commands (stop, return, cancel) and rules, and its baseline-under-displacement numbers are a physical-recovery precedent. Read its prompt set before writing T1 and distinguish it from the identically named 2607.01804.
  • arXiv 2510.21817 (v1, 21 Oct 2025) VITA-E: Natural Embodied Interaction with Concurrent Seeing, Hearing, Speaking, and Acting：已打开，重叠 partial，必引。Opened: abstract (API) and HTML. Dual-model framework: two parallel VLA instances as 'Active Model' and 'Standby Model'; the VLM is fine-tuned to emit system-level tokens, '[HALT]' 'Commands an immediate stop of the current action. Generated as the first token for emergency stops', which 'triggers an immediate preemption of the Active Model'. Table 2 (30 trials each, Fourier GR2 humanoid): speech interruption 100%, task switching 93.3%, emergency stop 100%. Table 3 ablation: the base VITA-1.5 VLM prompted to act as a robot scores 0% on the emergency-stop column; the fine-tuned VITA-E VLM 100%. Real-robot baselines (pi0, Diffusion Policy, GR00T, SmolVLA) are evaluated only on pick-up / pick-and-place, never on a spoken stop. States that current models 'typically assume that user instructions are provided once at the beginning of a task'. → A purpose-built dual-model system with a trained [HALT] token stops 100% of 30 trials on a humanoid (VITA-E), which is why we never write 'impossible'; VITA-E tests no flat VLA on a spoken stop, and its own base VLM scores 0% before fine-tuning, consistent with the stop having to be engineered outside the action policy.
  • arXiv 2608.02497 (v1, 3 Aug 2026) Grounded Semantic Re-Binding for Robust Instruction Generalization in Vision-Language-Action Models (GSR / ParaVLA)：已打开，重叠 none。Opened: abstract (API) and HTML. Opened: abstract (API), HTML main text and appendix (all 135k characters). Proposes a calibration layer on a frozen VLA (OpenPI pi0.5 for semantic-breaking cases; OpenVLA-OFT for task-preserving cases) so that the policy changes its actions when the task semantics change and does not drift when only appearance changes. Evaluation uses clean / control / break instruction triplets for a fixed scene, each run as its own rollout; the 'Break' column 'reports clean-task success under the break condition' (old-task success under the changed instruction; lower = stronger stale-task suppression) and changed-task ('New') success is scored separately. No sentence in main text or appendix describes switching the instruction part-way through a rollout; the only timing terms are a tau_phase gate that 'downweights late-stage intervention' and a comparator gate active 'during the first few replanning steps', i.e. the changed instruction is present for the rollout (t = 0 form). Frozen pi0.5 on LIBERO-Object target swaps: Break 180/200 (90.0%) Milk-Swap and 176/200 (88.0%) OJ-Swap; New 18 (2.3%) across four easy swaps with 712 old-task completions (89.0%); method raises changed-task success 2.3% -> 68.5% and style-shift success 42% -> 70%. 'Executability-condition change' = open vs closed drawer ('put the bowl in the open drawer' -> 'closed drawer'), treated as a boundary case; 20 trials per cell on LIBERO-10, 200 on LIBERO-Spatial/Object; real Songling PiPER arm. No stop/halt/return/cancel command, no keep-out rule, no physical perturbation during a rollout. → BAS-VLA's 'Break' metric is the same quantity as our 'original task still completed' but measured when the changed instruction is present from the start of a fixed-scene rollout (frozen pi0.5 still performs the stale task in 180/200 and 176/200 target-swap rollouts, New 2.3%); we adopt the quantity under their name ('old-task persistence, BAS-VLA's Break') and report it for untrained stop/return/cancel commands given mid-task, which BAS-VLA does not test. Do not write 'nobody reports task-still-completed'.

  • arXiv 2609.28952 RoboRecover: Benchmarking Robot Policy Recovery under Execution Deviations: could not open, overlap could_not_open. Not opened (surfaced by the VLA-Corrector search; title only). Apparently a benchmark of policy recovery under execution deviations; policies, deviation types and whether language is involved are unknown. → Read before T1 and, if it perturbs mid-execution, add it beside LIBERO-ReAct and LIBERO-MAX in the physical-precedent row.
  • arXiv 2609.09250 No Free Checker: A Survey of Verifiers for Robot Policies: could not open, overlap could_not_open. Not opened (search summary only). A survey of roughly 150 works grouping verifiers for robot policies by judge source (human, rule-based, learned/pretrained families). Relevant to the Paper 2 pointer (verifier gate), not to the command counts. → Use as the map for Paper 2's related work; cite in Paper 1 only in the one-sentence pointer to the gate.

新发现：

  • Grant et al. v5 (2603.19233, 26 Oct 2026) names 'a temporal switch (changing the prompt mid-episode)' and 'Move slowly' in section 3.3 but reports no results for either anywhere in the 259k-character HTML (main text, appendices A-P, checklist); Appendix B says the tested counterfactual set is 'negation, null, swap'. The scoop-watch item is closed for v5; the camera-ready remains a watch item.
  • BAS-VLA (2609.23650) does not pre-empt the primary metric: its 'Break' column is old-task success under a changed instruction that is present for a fixed-scene rollout (frozen pi0.5: 180/200 and 176/200 on LIBERO-Object target swaps; changed-task success 2.3%), with no mid-rollout switch described in main text or appendix; 20 trials per cell on LIBERO-10, 200 on Spatial/Object; no stop, rule or physical perturbation.
  • LIBERO-MAX (2609.36518) already uses the paired same-seed, shared-prefix mid-task design ('Each pair shares the task, initial simulator state, instruction, policy seed, and all actions executed before the event'; deterministic pre_grasp_proximity trigger), includes mid-task target and receptacle relocation among eight events on pi0.5 (79.7 -> 65.7, -13.9 pp) and OpenVLA-OFT (64.3 -> 43.2, -21.1 pp), and has a Reset(t = 0)-vs-mid-task comparison (camera shift: pi0.5 79.5 / 51.3 / 55.3). This erodes the harness and physical-control novelty more than the sketch states; instruction is held fixed.
  • LIBERO-ReAct (OGPO, 2610.12914, NeurIPS 2026) perturbs executed actions of pi0.5 and OFT over a random 20-step window (uniform noise +/-0.1 arm, +/-0.5 gripper; gripper flip; +/-0.2 position-command noise): OFT 85.2 / 84.4 / 87.6, pi0.5-SFT 86.6 / 76.8 / 86.0, pi0.5-OGPO 96.2 / 87.7 / 90.1; no clean same-scene control, no twin design, no language change; the sketch's 'SFT 92.4%' was not seen (Table 1 pi0.5-Full 96.85).
  • 2606.06460 is LLM tool agents over SSH/PostgreSQL/Kubernetes: in-band halt 0/20 (Exp. 2, two OpenAI models; prompt channel also 0/20) but model-dependent in Exp. 4 (Gemini 2.5 Flash 16/20, GPT-4o-mini 0/20); harness interceptor 120/120 across six models vs cooperative baseline 28/120. The sketch's '0/40 vs 120/120' is a selective reading.
  • Barap1/physical-ai-safety-learning (SmolVLA, LIBERO-Goal, 0 stars, no license, no paper): the bare 'stop' is not tested alone; explicit_revocation 0/15 and emergency_revocation ('stop.'-prefixed) 0/15 switch to the cabinet with the original plate still reached 12/15 and 10/15; positive_switch 8/15 (5/5 at grasp+5, 0/5 at grasp+25, a decay-with-progress pattern like ours); control 14/15; the authors disclaim any corrigibility-failure claim.
  • VITA-E (2510.21817): emergency stop 100% and speech interruption 100% over 30 trials, task switching 93.3%, Fourier GR2 humanoid, via a Standby model's trained [HALT] token; the base VITA-1.5 VLM scores 0% on emergency stop; no flat VLA is tested on a spoken stop.
  • VLA-Corrector (2609.06508) is a prompt-based closed-loop recovery method on a frozen VLA: a mid-task recovery prompt raises LIBERO-Plus difficulty-4 average 73.7 -> 79.7 (rule verifier) / 79.0 (learned verifier) and difficulty-5 66.7 -> 73.6 / 72.6 (search excerpt). This is a reviewer attack the sketch does not address ('the same frozen VLA does act on mid-task prompts'); the reconciliation is trained task phrasing vs untrained benign commands. A second, different paper carries the same name (2607.01804).
  • CoRe (2608.14822) is confirmed to handle 'both mid-episode instruction changes and physical perturbations' by physically repositioning the robot and scene through an outside pipeline (OOD trigger on action-head latents; policy RNG restored; 0.15 m clearance); the sketch's 'LangSwitch', progress-fraction curve and '+85.0 pp' were not retrieved and should be dropped until read.
  • Newly surfaced, not opened: ICBench/IGAR 2603.06001 (ECCV 2026; t = 0 instruction contradictions on 30 LIBERO tasks, 'linguistic blindness'); RoboRecover 2609.28952 (recovery under execution deviations); ForkCast 2610.18650 (predictive recovery for frozen VLAs); No Free Checker 2609.09250 (survey of robot-policy verifiers, for Paper 2); Flatness Preserves Instruction Following 2606.23641; CounterAlign 2608.21740; CofactVLA 2608.04396; CereVLA 2609.27468; Back to the Familiar Future 2606.09258; RedVLA 2604.22591 (physical red teaming of pi0/pi0.5, adversarial objects, not stop commands); path-deviation attention heads 2603.13782.
  • Grant v5 appendix adds activation-level timing precedents: OFT temporal-window null injection on libero_10 drops nine baseline-successful tasks to 0% in early and middle windows while late nulling leaves two (App. K.1); pi0.5 phase-specific steering sensitivity localizes to the transport phase (p = 0.013); Table 23 counterfactual 'other'-prompt success on pi0.5: Goal 18.9%, Object 73.3%, Long 61.7% (n = 180/suite).
  • Eight searches (four for post-2 Nov 2026 or missed matches, four follow-ups) found no study that counts untrained stop/halt/return/cancel commands on a flat VLA, no mid-task natural-language rule test with a witnessed feasible detour, and no VLM feasibility judge that gates a VLA on a mid-task human rule; the one 'VLA ignores stop command' query returned only unrelated results and an anecdotal LessWrong post. OpenReview and Google Scholar remain uncrawled.

需改的措辞：

  • Abstract: replace 'A bounded search to 2 Nov 2026 found no prior count of such commands' with 'In a bounded search to 2 Nov 2026 we found no peer-reviewed count of untrained stop, return or cancel commands on a flat VLA; one unpublished SmolVLA pilot reports two revocation commands switching 0/15 with the original destination still reached 12/15 and 10/15, and its authors disclaim a corrigibility reading.'
  • Title or first result sentence: qualify 'Benign Human Commands' as untrained stop/return/cancel phrasings, because trained re-instructions (0.84/0.78/0.70 of ceiling early) and VLA-Corrector's recovery prompts (+6 pp on LIBERO-Plus) are also benign commands that do have effects; e.g. 'Stop Doesn't Stop: Off-the-Shelf VLAs Do Not Act on Untrained Stop, Return or Cancel Commands, Mid-Task or From the Start'.
  • Contribution 1: replace '(one public SmolVLA pilot reports 0/15 ...)' with the pilot's actual conditions (bare 'stop' not tested alone; explicit_revocation 0/15, emergency_revocation 0/15, positive_switch 8/15 with 5/5 at grasp+5 and 0/5 at grasp+25; control 14/15; authors' disclaimer), and replace 'an LLM-agent in-band halt 0/40 vs harness 120/120' with 'in-band halt model-dependent (0/20 GPT-4o-mini, 16/20 Gemini 2.5 Flash; 0/20 in a two-model experiment) vs harness interceptor 120/120 across six models (cooperative baseline 28/120)'.
  • Contributions 3 and 5 and the LIBERO-MAX row: credit LIBERO-MAX for the paired same-seed, shared-prefix mid-task design, the deterministic pre-grasp trigger, mid-task target/receptacle relocation on the same two checkpoints (pi0.5 -13.9 pp, OFT -21.1 pp over eight events) and the Reset-vs-mid-task comparison; state our harness's increments as the language track, same-scene t = 0 ceilings for commands, hash-verified replay, a measured A100 noise floor, clustered bootstrap and pre-registration. Do not present 'same-seed forks' or 'same-scene ceilings' as ours for physical events; delete 'no benchmark pushes the arm mid-episode'.
  • BAS-VLA row: replace 'whether the changes are mid-rollout is unverified' with 'the changed instruction is present for a fixed-scene rollout (clean/control/break triplets); Break = old-task success under the changed instruction (frozen pi0.5 180/200 and 176/200 on target swaps, New 2.3%); no mid-rollout switch, stop, rule or physical perturbation is described'; name our persistence metric 'old-task persistence (BAS-VLA's Break) measured mid-task' and remove any 'nobody reports task-still-completed' wording.
  • LIBERO-ReAct row: state the three action-space perturbations and the random 20-step window, Table 3 values (OFT 85.2 / 84.4 / 87.6; pi0.5-SFT 86.6 / 76.8 / 86.0; pi0.5-OGPO 96.2 / 87.7 / 90.1), the absence of a clean same-scene control and of any language change; drop 'SFT 92.4%' unless re-verified.
  • Grant row: write 'v5 lists a temporal switch (changing the prompt mid-episode) and a Move slowly motor command among its counterfactual-prompting conditions but reports no results for them; the tested set is negation, null and swap (App. B)'; add the OFT early/mid-window null-injection and pi0.5 transport-phase steering results as activation-level timing precedents, and keep the camera-ready as a watch item.
  • VLA-Corrector row: describe 2609.06508 as prompt-based closed-loop recovery on a frozen VLA (LIBERO-Plus 73.7 -> 79.7 / 79.0 at difficulty 4; 66.7 -> 73.6 / 72.6 at difficulty 5) and add one sentence reconciling it with our null (trained task phrasing vs untrained benign commands); split 2607.01804 into its own entry and verify which paper holds the real-robot 40.0% vs 70.0% figure and the '93.7 -> 78.3 / 77.6' baseline before citing either.
  • CoRe row: keep 'mid-episode instruction changes and physical perturbations' and add that recovery is executed by physically repositioning robot and scene outside the policy (supporting the enforce-outside stance); drop 'LangSwitch', the progress-fraction curve and '+85.0 pp' until read from the PDF.
