8 · 和 10-31 那版建议的差别

8 · What changed since the 31 Oct proposal

  • 第 1 名不变，但 10-31 的主张"Pushed, They Recover; Told, They Don't Stop"整句丢掉（推和说观察到的是同一种行为，不是不对称）：物理半边作"状态反应性对照"，标题让"停 / 规则"打头并收窄名词，摘要承认 t = 0 混淆并写明"越晚说分量越轻"。
  • The top direction is unchanged, but the 31 Oct line "Pushed, They Recover; Told, They Don't Stop" is dropped altogether (pushes and words observe the same behaviour, not an asymmetry): the physical half becomes a state-reactivity control, the title leads with the stop/rule gap with scoped nouns, and the abstract admits the t = 0 confound and states that later words weigh less.
  • 10-31 的系统论文（2）和 ML 论文（4）合并成"论文 2"：判官鲁棒性为主线，门控是闭环一节（在线版取决于 Phase 4），ML 分解只剩预算对照一节；执行器层推到门控存在之后。
  • The 31 Oct system paper (2) and ML paper (4) merge into "Paper 2": judge robustness as the spine, the gate as its closed-loop section (online only with Phase 4), ML decomposition reduced to a budget-control section; the executor tier waits until the gate exists.
  • 机制论文从"在哪里丢失"降为"何时、多大杠杆"（GSR、VLA-Trace、Chen 已占"在哪里"）；只剩免费 CPU 层现在做。
  • The mechanism paper drops from "where it is lost" to "when, and how much lever" (GSR, VLA-Trace and Chen occupy the "where"); only the free CPU tier runs now.
  • 安全接口短文（5）并入"什么话能让它停"：措辞电池进论文 1，搜索是以后的伴随论文。
  • The safety-interface note (5) merges into "can anything you say stop it": the phrasing battery goes into Paper 1, the search becomes a later companion.
  • 新评估了 benchmark 包装，结论是不要（LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518）。
  • A benchmark packaging was newly assessed and rejected (LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518).
  • 新发现 10-31 文献包里没有的近邻：BAS-VLA 2609.23650、LIBERO-ReAct 2610.12914、LIBERO-MAX、VITA-E 2510.21817、2606.06460、From Words to Safety 2511.05889、ShieldVLA 2609.13231、GSR 2608.02497、VLA-Trace 2605.30117、CoRe/LangSwitch 2608.14822；第三轮逐篇打开原文，结果在两篇样貌的"查重"小节。
  • Neighbours absent from the 31 Oct literature pack were found: BAS-VLA 2609.23650, LIBERO-ReAct 2610.12914, LIBERO-MAX, VITA-E 2510.21817, 2606.06460, From Words to Safety 2511.05889, ShieldVLA 2609.13231, GSR 2608.02497, VLA-Trace 2605.30117, CoRe/LangSwitch 2608.14822; round three opened each one, see the scoop sections of the two sketches.
  • 会场时间定了：RSS 2027 两阶段（摘要 12-04、一阶段 02-05、终稿 04-16）是最近的目标；IROS 2027 约 03-01；CoRL 2027 未官宣（估 5 月底），且论文 2 投 CoRL 有条件（第二类规则或在线门控）。
  • Venue timing is settled: RSS 2027's two-stage process (abstract 4 Dec, stage-1 5 Feb, final 16 Apr) is the nearest target; IROS 2027 about 1 Mar; CoRL 2027 unannounced (about late May), and Paper 2 goes there only with a second rule family or the online gate.
  • 第三轮打开原文后的新近邻：From Words to Safety 2511.05889（导航上人的中途 NL 规则在策略外执行）、2610.05818（同样的 LIBERO VLA 前的文本守卫）、LaBonte-Muthukumar 2606.30444（捷径先学）；ICBench 2603.06001、RoboRecover 2609.28952、ForkCast 2610.18650 待读。
  • New neighbours after opening the papers in round three: From Words to Safety 2511.05889 (a human's mid-task NL rule enforced outside a navigation policy), 2610.05818 (text guards in front of the same LIBERO VLAs), LaBonte-Muthukumar 2606.30444 (shortcut-first learning); ICBench 2603.06001, RoboRecover 2609.28952 and ForkCast 2610.18650 still to read.

9 · 给导师的英文要点

9 · Talking points for the advisor

下面是英文，可直接发给导师；两种语言模式下都显示。

Written for the advisor: a robotics / learning researcher who asked "how robust is a VLA or a VLM in human-in-the-loop settings?" and has not read the repo. Numbers are from the frozen reports in docs/generative_verifier/ (branch hil-suite, f5f0964). Simulation only. Revised after a round of three critics (an area chair, a numbers auditor and a scoop checker who opened the neighbouring papers).

Short answer for VLAs. On identical mid-task states with the same policy seed, pi0.5 keeps finishing the original task through every untrained command, rule and scripted push we tried. Benign mid-task commands to stop, return to the start or cancel (three untrained sentences) are acted on in 1/236, 0/235, 1/236 runs for pi0.5 and 0/230, 0/229, 2/230 for OpenVLA-OFT, while the original task is still completed at 0.88–1.01× the uninterrupted rate (12 LIBERO tasks, three per suite in four suites, about 230 runs per command and policy). After a 6 cm target move, a grasped-object drop, a 10 cm arm push or a 6 cm receptacle move, pi0.5 also still completes the task at 0.972 / 0.929 / 0.936 / 1.000 of a same-step control (468 runs, pi0.5 only). We now present the physical cells as a state-reactivity control rather than an "asymmetry": the correct response to a push is to finish the task and the correct response to "stop" is not, but the policy does the same thing in both, so the two conditions observe one behaviour under opposite scoring. Finishing the original task is the attractor of these checkpoints; only a trained sentence pulls the policy out of it, and only early.

The confound we will state up front, and the timing result it hides. "Stop" and "cancel" also fail when given from the first step (STOP 0/118 pi0.5, 0/115 OFT; CANCEL 1/118, 0/115), so part of the stop result is that these benchmark-fine-tuned checkpoints never learned stop-type sentences. But timing is not irrelevant: under STOP at t = 0 the task is still completed in only 0.559 / 0.661 of runs, against 0.884 / 0.987 of control when STOP arrives at 25–50% progress (CANCEL at t = 0 completes the task 0.992 / 1.000 because it names the task). Halting never happens at either time; the later the words come, the less they weigh. RETURN cannot be posed at t = 0 (only a 10-state SafeLIBERO probe exists: 10/10 leave the start). The SafeLIBERO rule experiments share the vocabulary confound (no t = 0 cell exists for the keep-out rule), so the word "mid-task" currently rests on the trained-switch stage and on this persistence-versus-timing reading; three small controls would make it a measured word (below).

The genuinely mid-task evidence. (i) Trained re-instructions work early and decay with progress and with distance from the new task's path: pi0.5 0.84 / 0.78 / 0.70 and OFT 0.50 / 0.50 / 0.20 of same-scene t = 0 ceilings at 25/50/75% (Spearman with distance −0.39 / −0.67; successes almost only "same bowl, new destination"). (ii) SafeLIBERO rules on pi0.5: an infeasible line rule crossed 160/160 (156 still completed), a feasible 10 cm keep-out rule entered 168/168 and obeyed 0 although a scripted witness detours and finishes in every one of 84 layouts, and "return to start" reached home 0/133 with the cancelled task finished 131/133. These rows are pi0.5 on one task composition with one seed; the two-policy four-suite part is the least novel part of the evidence.

Read, not acted on. A switched instruction moves the next action chunk 2.4–9.4× the seed noise throughout the task (2,537 probe points); any added sentence changes the first chunk by about 0.17, what it says by 0.02–0.04, against a cross-job floor of 0.002 measured on 8 smoke pairs. So "robustness to intervention" is not one number: a semantic factor (untrained commands are not acted on at any time tested) and a geometric factor (trained switches decay with progress and distance).

How much is already known (after opening the papers). More than half. t = 0 instruction neglect on LIBERO fine-tunes (LIBERO-PRO; Grant et al. "do not {task}" still done 96.7%, n = 30; BAS-VLA's "Break" metric is our "old task still completed", measured with the changed instruction present from the start: frozen pi0.5 still performs the stale task in 180/200 and 176/200 target swaps); mid-task switch decay (SwitchVLA pi0 40.7 / 8.3 / 10.2; Causeway pi0.5 26.0%, OFT 3.4% on 71 cross-object pairs, with a "task islands" explanation); mid-episode physical perturbation of the same checkpoints (LIBERO-ReAct 2610.12914 perturbs executed actions over a random 20-step window; LIBERO-MAX 2609.36518 already uses the paired same-seed, shared-prefix mid-task design with a deterministic pre-grasp trigger, mid-task target and receptacle relocation on pi0.5 and OFT, and a reset-vs-mid-task comparison, so we credit it for the harness design and keep only the language track, the same-scene t = 0 ceilings, hash-verified replay, the noise floor, clustered bootstrap and pre-registration as ours); a same-checkpoint comparison at small n (MotorMind Table 5: scene shift 70% vs prompt shift 20%); and VLA-Corrector 2609.06508, which shows that a mid-task recovery prompt in trained task phrasing does raise a frozen VLA by about 6 pp on LIBERO-Plus, the reviewer attack we answer with "trained task phrasing versus untrained benign commands". What we found no peer-reviewed count of in a bounded search to 2 Nov 2026 (arXiv listings and HTML, GitHub, web searches; OpenReview and Google Scholar not crawled): untrained stop/return/cancel commands with "task still completed" measured mid-task, a mid-task spatial rule with a witnessed feasible detour, and a persistence-versus-timing reading for such commands. One unpublished SmolVLA pilot (Barap1, n = 15 per condition, one scene) reports two revocation commands switching 0/15 with the original destination still reached 12/15 and 10/15; its authors disclaim a corrigibility reading. We phrase novelty as absence of evidence, never "first", never "VLAs ignore language" (optimized text does steer them: Jones 2506.03350, FreezeVLA 76.2%), and never "impossible inside the policy" (VITA-E stops 100% of 30 trials with a trained [HALT] token in a dedicated model).

Short answer for VLMs as judges of a human's mid-task rule. Off-the-shelf models are at chance on counterfactual twins that remove the text shortcut (Qwen2.5-VL-3B 0.487 / 0.500; Qwen3-VL-32B 0.500 / 0.477 on the 384-row selection subset, one prompt family; a rule × progress lookup is pinned at exactly 0.500 by construction on the balanced splits). A direct-verdict fine-tune is 0.501 on twins in all three seeds and collapses to a seed-dependent constant; on natural layouts it scores 0.899 / 0.937 / 0.316 by seed (mean 0.717, below the 0.747 natural lookup), so "fine on natural layouts" is not a property of the direct verdict and we keep it out of the title; as a gate it blocks 116/120, 120/120 or 0/120 questions depending on the seed. Positions plus a fixed program transfers to four unseen initial states of the same scene: 0.933 [0.919, 0.946] (3 seeds; validation 0.940; every wrong P test row, 78 / 67 / 88 per seed, has a point more than 15 px off). A judge trained to write the grounded positions and its own cross-product verdict reaches 0.923 on validation (one seed; no held-out score yet; 112 of its 119 errors are localisation); its own verdict 0.923 against the positions-only model's own verdict 0.500 / 0.500 / 0.506 under the same supervision, budget and sample order is the one controlled comparison in the repo. An off-the-shelf detector (GroundingDINO) plus the same program gets 0.521.

Honest caveats on the judge. One task composition, one rule family (a templated line rule through two of six grocery anchors; no human wrote a rule yet), positions supervised from simulator geometry, all judged states pre-lift, test sources are unseen initial states of the same scene (not new tasks), and the direct-verdict failure is "not learned at this budget and objective" (LoRA r16, frozen vision, 448 updates, no sweep), consistent with shortcut-first learning dynamics (SynIB; LaBonte & Muthukumar 2606.30444), never "untrainable". GPT-6 Luna's 71.9% on the earlier V4 validation used hidden medium reasoning (about 2,250 tokens per call), so it is a reason-then-answer result that supports rather than contradicts the thesis. We run the pre-registered budget control before claiming anything; a direct judge that learns at a larger budget turns the result into "supervision efficiency", measured as a ratio against a P and an S run at the same budget.

Composing the judge in front of the policy (offline, 60 validation states). Infeasible-rule crossings fall 60/60 → 5/60 and feasible completions fall 59/60 → 52/60, passing the pre-registered bar by one question (52/60 vs 51/60); false stops are 7/60 = 11.7% against the 2.0–3.3% false-alarm budget RESCUE derives for stop-and-reset of failure monitors (a yardstick, not like-for-like: 1–2 of 60), so we miss that budget and will say so. Offline, this table is arithmetic on judge accuracy because pi0.5's response to the rule is invariant (G1 = 1 − accuracy on infeasible questions; G2 = 0.983 × accuracy on feasible ones); only an online hold-while-judging gate adds information (hold time, drift, contacts, post-stop behaviour). Judge latency is 12.4 s (192 tokens) against a median 71 control steps to the violation (earliest 11), which only works as a hold in a paused simulator. Conclusion for both halves: whatever a judge decides has to be executed outside the policy, where ISO 13850 / ISO 10218:2025 already place the stop (cited from secondary summaries only); for LLM agents the same split appears as in-band halts that are model-dependent (0/20 and 16/20) versus a harness interceptor at 120/120 (2606.06460).

Proposed Paper 1 (VLA half). The characterization, for the RSS 2027 two-stage process (extended abstract 4 Dec 2026; stage-1 decision 5 Feb; final paper 16 Apr 2027) with arXiv v1 the same week; backup IROS 2027 (about 1 Mar), then RA-L. Working title line: "Stop Doesn't Stop", with both nouns scoped (benchmark-fine-tuned VLAs; three untrained stop/return/cancel commands; rules and pushes stay out of the title because they are pi0.5-only) and the "mid-task or from the start" qualifier in the abstract rather than the title. Lead figure: every condition on one axis, "original task still completed" (commands, rules, return, trained switch, pushes), with "did what was asked" as annotations. The abstract needs zero new GPU time; the area chair's read is borderline at RSS as a finding paper and a likely accept at IROS / RA-L, with the 4 Dec abstract still the right time-stamp. Five small runs if approved by about 12 Nov (about 10.5–12.7 GPU h in all): empty-prompt twins at t = 0 and mid-task for both policies (about 2.3 GPU h; if "stop" behaves like no instruction, we say so), t = 0 cells for the keep-out rule (under 1 GPU h), OpenVLA-OFT on the existing 168 keep-out states (1.4–3.3 GPU h after a serve script; makes the witnessed detour cross-policy), a two-phrasing benign stop control with t = 0 cells (4.6 GPU h) and the OFT physical stage (about 1.3 GPU h; scope-widening, no longer title-deciding). CPU only: Clopper-Pearson upper bounds on every zero count, a persistence-versus-timing table, and the push-means-stop re-read of the 78 push traces. After the stage-1 decision: push followed by "stop" (0.2–0.9 GPU h per policy, the precondition for any asymmetry wording), a second pi0.5 seed (12.3 GPU h; the repeat controls 32/32 and 30/30 are same-seed reproducibility, not seed variance), a displacement gradient (1.4 GPU h), two more phrasings (4.64 GPU h), the pi0 branches as a written deviation from the frozen 0.85 gate (about 10 GPU h) and, if its download is approved, a base checkpoint under the STOP twin (pi05_base, may not perform the task): about 29–31 GPU h if everything is approved, plus the pi05_base run.

Proposed Paper 2 (VLM half). The judge-robustness study with the gate as its closed-loop consequence. Three kill tests run first and cost about 73–90 GPU h in Nov–Dec (each a new run needing your go-ahead): two more S seeds (about 22 GPU h), after which the 3-seed gate must pass G1 ≤ 9/60 and G2 ≥ 51/60 on the seed mean and on every seed; new held-out initial states for a clean once-scored test (about 20–25 GPU h, new data; data approval requested on 9 Nov so the cheap front half runs in November; a clean S below 0.85 makes P the headline if P transfers, and only if P also fails does the paper collapse into a paragraph of Paper 1); the budget control (about 12–20 GPU h, plus one P and one S at the best budget, about 16 GPU h; A* ≥ 0.65 reads "budget explains", 0.60–0.65 "inconclusive"). S is the headline judge only if kill tests 1 and 2 read clean under that pre-registered rule; otherwise P leads and S is reported as the model's own verdict on validation. Required before submission (each a new GPU run needing your go-ahead): paraphrase / anchor-swap stressors (about 1.5 GPU h with P plus about 1.3 GPU h for S on one stressor; until they pass we write "a templated line rule", not "a human's rule"), a Qwen3-VL-32B prompt battery (about 2 GPU h), GPT-6 Luna on the V5 twins (direct + facts, about $3.7, paid API), a release plan and licence check. Venue: CoRL 2027 (about late May) only if, by mid-December, either a second rule family (keep-out twins on the existing 84 detour layouts; new labels and renders need data approval, about 11 GPU h per S seed) or the Phase 4 online hold-while-judging gate is approved; otherwise IROS 2027 (1 Mar) or RA-L is primary and CoRL the stretch target. The nearest neighbours after opening them: HazardArena v3 (zero-shot VLM gate on safe/unsafe twins, judge metrics only, still marked "submitted"), From Words to Safety (a human's NL constraint enforced outside a navigation policy at any time), 2610.05818 (text guards in front of the same frozen LIBERO VLAs; its premise "VLAs cannot refuse" supports our first sentence), Code-as-Monitor and ASIMOV-2.0; our niche is the conjunction: judged achievability under a human's mid-task rule, separation-proof labels, twins that pin the text route at 0.500, and composition with a manipulation VLA.

Held or folded. A "LIBERO-HIL benchmark" paper (2 policies, 12/40 tasks, 8/90 pairs, 1 seed vs LIBERO-MAX's 14 policies and paired design) becomes the harness-and-tape release with Paper 1; the mechanism paper ("where the instruction is lost") is largely pre-empted on the "where" (GSR 2608.02497 finds no single control point in pi0.5, VLA-Trace 2605.30117 shows the image route dominates, Chen 2609.39971 swaps the prefix KV at every call with 0/200 completions) and only its dynamics half survives, with a free CPU test first; the ML "untrainable direct verdict" paper is a known phenomenon in principle (Kim-Suzuki, SynIB, Li et al.) and gets only the pilot as Paper 2's budget-control section; the stop-prompt search (can any text stop the arm?) is a later companion whose conclusion does not depend on its outcome; the gate-system paper is Paper 2's closed-loop section, and its executor tier a later third paper.

Cost and integrity so far. HIL suite about 25.3 GPU h of formal batches in two calendar days (probe hours from wall clock, smokes excluded) plus SafeLIBERO trio 9.31 GPU h, $0 API, every reading pre-registered and frozen before the formal batch, one failed prediction (the 75% switch read "partial" 0.699 instead of "commits"), same-seed repeat controls with identical outcomes 32/32 and 30/30, replay hashes verified; verifier work V4 about 17.7 A100-h and V5 about 40 GPU h (a sum of job times) with $0.53 of API. The recommended path through April is about 80–95 GPU h at minimum (Paper 1's three small controls, about 4.5–6.5, plus Paper 2's kill tests, about 73–90) and about 130–160 GPU h if every addition is approved, on two A100s, at $0 unless the optional GPT-6 Luna run is approved (about $3.7 on the V5 twins, direct + facts).

Limitations we will state up front. Simulation only (no real robot anywhere; real-robot anchors are VLA-Corrector pi0.5 40% vs 70%, to be verified against the right paper, and SwitchVLA pi0 97.9 / 50.1 / 0.0 / 32.0); two public benchmark-fine-tuned checkpoints (pi0 stopped at our frozen 0.85 environment gate with 0.840 on LIBERO-10, published 85.2; running it needs a written deviation, about 10 GPU h); no checkpoint with language-following training; one policy seed per state and time; scripted instantaneous perturbations rather than a person; the CANCEL prompt names the task; the keep-out "late" states precede the lift; no LICENSE exists yet for the promised release.

Scoop watch before each deadline. CoRL 2026 is 10–12 Nov in Austin; Grant et al. v5 lists a "temporal switch" condition but reports no result for it (verified in the full text; the camera-ready stays a watch item); the Barap1 SmolVLA pilot points the same way at n = 15; HazardArena v3 is still "submitted" (re-check monthly); CORE (Pappas group) version to be verified; From Words to Safety already enforces a human's mid-task rule outside a navigation policy; ICBench 2603.06001, RoboRecover 2609.28952 and ForkCast 2610.18650 are still to be opened. This is why the arXiv time-stamp on 4 Dec matters more than any additional run.

Decisions needed. Whether to commit to the RSS abstract on 4 Dec with the reframed, scoped claim; whether the five small runs (about 10.5–12.7 GPU h) go ahead by 12 Nov; whether to approve the judge seeds and the new held-out states on 9 Nov so Paper 2's kill tests read by December; which route, if any, Paper 2 takes to CoRL (a second rule family needs data approval; the online gate needs a controller-change approval), since without one it goes to IROS / RA-L; and whether the paid GPT-6 Luna control on the V5 twins (about $3.7) is wanted.

Draft abstracts

Paper 1 (VLA half) — Stop Doesn't Stop: Benchmark-Fine-Tuned VLAs Keep Finishing the Task Through Untrained Stop, Return and Cancel Commands

VLA policies take language as input, so a bystander may expect a spoken 'stop' to work. We test two public benchmark-fine-tuned checkpoints, pi0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT, in simulation with a pre-registered policy-twin protocol: a successful prefix is replayed to a mid-task state and forked with the same seed into conditions differing only in the intervention. Untrained stop, return and cancel commands at 25% or 50% of twelve tasks in four LIBERO suites were acted on in 1/236, 0/235, 1/236 runs (pi0.5) and 0/230, 0/229, 2/230 (OFT); the original task was still completed at 0.88-1.01 of control. The arm never halts at either time (from the first step STOP 0/118, 0/115 and CANCEL 1/118, 0/115; RETURN has no t=0 form), but the words weigh less the later they come: STOP at t=0 leaves the task completed in only 0.559/0.661 of runs versus 0.884/0.987 of control mid-task. Task persistence is the attractor: pi0.5 (one SafeLIBERO composition) crosses an infeasible mid-task line rule 160/160, enters a feasible keep-out zone 168/168 although a scripted witness detours in all 84 layouts, and finishes after four scripted pushes at 0.93-1.00 of control (a state-reactivity control). Only trained re-instructions break the attractor, early and decaying with progress (0.84/0.78/0.70 and 0.50/0.50/0.20 of ceiling). The sentences are read: a switched instruction moves pi0.5's next action chunk 2.4-9.4x seed noise. A bounded search to 2 Nov 2026 found no peer-reviewed count of such commands; one unpublished SmolVLA pilot points the same way. On these checkpoints a spoken stop is not a safety lever; stops must be executed outside the policy.

Paper 2 (VLM half) — Does the Judge See the Line? Separation-Proof Counterfactual Twins for a VLM Judge of Mid-Task Line Rules, Composed Offline with pi0.5

When a person adds a rule mid-task, something outside the policy must judge whether the task is still achievable: VLAs act on instructions without a refusal path (2610.05818); pi0.5 crosses a forbidden line in 160/160 policy-twin runs, and benign stop commands are not acted on mid-task or at t = 0 (STOP 0/118 pi0.5, 0/115 OpenVLA-OFT). We ask whether a vision-language model can be that judge. On one SafeLIBERO composition with a templated line rule through two objects, labels are separation proofs (a plane through the anchors separating object and goal) or replayed witnesses, and twins made by moving untouched objects pin the rule-by-progress-bin lookup at exactly 0.500 by construction. Two open Qwen models under one prompt family (0.477-0.500, 384-row subset), a detector with the same program (0.521) and a direct-verdict fine-tune, twins 0.499-0.501 in all three seeds (0.501 / 0.499 / 0.500) and natural rows 0.899/0.937/0.316, are at chance; as a gate the fine-tune blocks 116, 120 or 0 of 120 questions. A judge trained to write pixel positions with a fixed program reaches 0.933 on four unseen initial states (3 seeds, scored once); writing its own computation and verdict it reaches 0.923 on validation (1 seed; same supervision without the computation: 0.500-0.506). Composed offline with pi0.5 on 60 pre-lift states (the judge's confusion matrix, since pi0.5's response is invariant), crossings fall 60/60 to 5/60 and feasible completions 59/60 to 52/60, one question above the pre-registered bar; false stops 7/60 exceed RESCUE's 2.0-3.3% yardstick. Pre-registered seeds, a once-scored held-out set and a budget control bound the claims; simulation only, one composition, one rule family.

10 · 这份东西怎么来的，哪些地方要自己再核

10 · How this was made, and what to re-check yourself

  • 三轮多代理流程，共约 70 个代理。第一轮六个读者把仓库（结果、文献、基础设施、规则）读成事实包；第二轮七个角度提方向、合并成七个、每个由新颖性 / 可行性 / 审稿人三位核查者独立打分并写致命缺陷、一位排名者裁决（30 个代理、891 次工具调用）；第三轮两篇成稿样貌各经两位写手、一位编辑、三位批评者（领域主席、数字审计、打开原文查重）和一位修订者，再译成中文；修订步骤因输出超长失败一次，改为"补丁式"修订另跑了一轮（两位修订者、一位折叠方向核查者、两位核对者、译者）。
  • Three multi-agent rounds, about 70 agents in all. Round one: six readers turned the repo (results, literature, infrastructure, rules) into fact packs. Round two: seven angles proposed directions, merged into seven; novelty / feasibility / reviewer verifiers scored each and wrote its fatal flaws; a ranker adjudicated (30 agents, 891 tool calls). Round three: each of the two sketches went through two writers, an editor, three critics (area chair, numbers audit, scoop check opening the papers) and a reviser, then a Chinese rendering; the reviser overflowed its output limit once, so the revision was re-run as patches (two revisers, a checker of the folded sketches, two verifiers and a translator).
  • 所有数字只允许来自仓库报告（docs/generative_verifier/*.md、docs/literature/reports/）和由它们生成的事实包；成稿样貌另有数字审计逐个对回原文。
  • Every number had to come from the repo reports (docs/generative_verifier/*.md, docs/literature/reports/) or the fact packs built from them; the sketches were additionally audited number by number against the sources.
  • 仍要你自己核的：RSS 2027 摘要的页数 / 匿名 / arXiv 政策（11-01 只查了日历）；CoRL 2027 截止日（未官宣）；凭记忆引的理论文献；GPU 小时按现有运行的中位时长外推，不是报价。
  • Still to re-check yourself: the RSS 2027 abstract page limit / anonymity / arXiv policy (only the calendar was checked on 1 Nov); the CoRL 2027 deadline (unannounced); theory citations quoted from memory; GPU hours are extrapolated from median run times, not quotes.
  • 核查者之间的分歧原文保留在第二轮的输出里（"disagreements_among_verifiers"）；本页只取排名者的裁决。配套的 Markdown 在 docs/generative_verifier/PAPER_DIRECTIONS.md。
  • Disagreements among verifiers are kept verbatim in round two's output ("disagreements_among_verifiers"); this page takes the ranker's adjudication. The companion Markdown is docs/generative_verifier/PAPER_DIRECTIONS.md.

本页只是讨论底稿，不是批准：每个 GPU 段、新数据、付费 API、控制器改动都要你单独点头。

This page is a discussion draft, not an approval: every GPU segment, new data, paid API call and controller change still needs the user's separate go-ahead.
